# Resource and compliance map

Generated by `scripts/compliance/render_compliance.py` from the [inventory](aws-compliance-inventory.json) and the [Security Hub CSPM control catalog](data/securityhub-controls.json) (retrieved 2026-11-18). It answers two questions: which compliance codes each resource this library creates is held to, and which codes of the selected frameworks are not met in code, and why. The [CSV](resource-compliance-map.csv) carries the same rows.

Frameworks: AWS FSBP v1.0.0, CIS AWS Foundations v1.2.0, v1.4.0, v3.0.0 and v5.0.0, NIST SP 800-53 r5, NIST SP 800-171 r2, PCI DSS v3.2.1 and v4.0.1, AWS Resource Tagging, AI Security Best Practices and CRI Profile v2.2. Requirement codes come from AWS's mapping of each Security Hub control. FSBP, Tagging and AI Security have no codes of their own, so they show as membership. The AWS Control Tower service-managed standard is not applicable: the organization does not use Control Tower, and that standard can only be enabled from Control Tower.

Status: `PASS` met by default; `PARTIAL` met by default but overridable, opt-in, or met for part of the scope; `FAIL` not met; `REQUIRES_RUNTIME_VALIDATION` only a live account can show it; `NOT_SUPPORTED` the provider has no argument for it; `PROVIDER_MANAGED` AWS does it with no configuration; `NOT_APPLICABLE` the control does not apply to how the module uses the service. Where a control appears for several modules, the worst status counts. Meeting a control does not certify compliance with a framework.

## Part 2: controls not met in code, and why

Every control of the selected frameworks that evaluates something this library could provide, and is not `PASS`. Opt-in organization policies count as `FAIL` until they are enabled and attached.

| Control | Title | Frameworks | Status | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [APIGateway.1](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/apigateway-controls.html#apigateway-1) | API Gateway REST and WebSocket API execution logging should be enabled | FSBP, NIST 800-53, CRI 2.2 | `PARTIAL` | execution_logging.enabled = false turns it off; needs the account-level API Gateway CloudWatch Logs role |
| [APIGateway.2](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/apigateway-controls.html#apigateway-2) | API Gateway REST API stages should be configured to use SSL certificates for backend authentication | FSBP, NIST 800-53, NIST 800-171, CRI 2.2 | `PARTIAL` | Off unless client_certificate_id is set |
| [APIGateway.3](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/apigateway-controls.html#apigateway-3) | API Gateway REST API stages should have AWS X-Ray tracing enabled | FSBP, NIST 800-53, CRI 2.2 | `PARTIAL` | Overridable |
| [APIGateway.4](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/apigateway-controls.html#apigateway-4) | API Gateway should be associated with a WAF Web ACL | FSBP, NIST 800-53, CRI 2.2 | `PARTIAL` | Off unless waf.enabled is set |
| [APIGateway.8](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/apigateway-controls.html#apigateway-8) | API Gateway routes should specify an authorization type | FSBP, NIST 800-53, CRI 2.2 | `PARTIAL` | authorization = "NONE" is an explicit opt-out and fails APIGateway.8 |
| [APIGateway.11](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/apigateway-controls.html#apigateway-11) | API Gateway domain names should use recommended security policies | FSBP | `PARTIAL` | APIGateway.11 accepts only SecurityPolicy_\* policies, which cannot map HTTP APIs, so it fails HTTP API domains by design |
| [Account.1](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/account-controls.html#account-1) | Security contact information should be provided for an AWS account | FSBP, CIS 5.0.0, NIST 800-53, CRI 2.2 | `PARTIAL` | Off by default; single-account alternative to organization rules (mutually exclusive); detective only; Security Hub service-linked rules evaluate the same conditions |
| [Account.2](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/account-controls.html#account-2) | AWS accounts should be part of an AWS Organizations organization | NIST 800-53, CRI 2.2 | `PARTIAL` | Created and attached only when targets are set; nothing is restricted until then. |

## Part 3: controls for services this library does not create

These controls evaluate AWS services that no module here creates, so they are not applicable to this library. They apply to any account that runs those services outside these modules.
